# PoUW protocol panel (RTX PRO 6000, sm_120)

Generated 01 Oct 2026, 01:43Z by `internal/pouw/panel/panel.py` from `internal/pouw/panel/attempts.jsonl` (227 rows) and the evidence store's `kernel-attempt/v1` records (4 rows, written by the harness's `verify.py`). Owner: the sm_120 PoUW coordinator (bc-2aa33ad8).

**What is plotted.** Slowdown is the total, hashing included, over the fastest plain GEMM of the same precision on the same card and shape. Solid dots are measurements (with a run id), and the solid step line is the best measurement so far, carried to the line's latest attempt. Hollow markers on a dotted line are estimates built from measured components; faint markers are paper estimates with nothing measured behind them. Only attempts whose γ is at most 1% under the line's stated assumptions are plotted; an estimate whose γ is still being derived is plotted and marked in the log. A kernel change is a new attempt on the same line; a change of protocol or cryptography is a new version, plotted as its own line. A measured decode row counts only when timed as a dependent chain (the next A depends on this y). Prefill is m = n = k = 8,192; decode is m = 32, n = k = 8,192. 1× means no overhead; 5× is the pass line, not the goal.

**Absolute prefill times on node 2 read up to about 1.4% slow** (ops, 13:00Z). The software power cap fires on 65–98% of timed prefill items, at 298–443 W against the 600 W default limit, so SM clocks sit 1–4 steps (7–30 MHz) under the 2,100 MHz lock. Ratios hold: arm and baseline alternate on one die and differ by at most 7 MHz. Decode items are never flagged, and no item is dropped for the flag.

![Prefill slowdown](/cursor/stores/bc-b729c175-2ef6-418e-98fe-10896709028b/media/pouw-slowdown-prefill.png)

![Decode slowdown](/cursor/stores/bc-b729c175-2ef6-418e-98fe-10896709028b/media/pouw-slowdown-decode.png)

*Off the plots: v2, v2-h1, v2-h2, v2-h3, aw-v2 (D, so not shown to be γ ≤ 1%: the assessor's charged floor is at least 0.946% packed, and v2's own provisional charge from atom 10 is 1.185% packed (`min-merge-search/deltav2_p8.0_n8192.json`), over the line). Off the plots: v2-hot (γ ≥ 0.951% packed on the full catalogue (t_c = 4), a rising floor, so not shown to be γ ≤ 1%; no-charge route pending fix (2)). Off the plots: v3, v3′ (headline row rated D, fix in design. The assessor rates `tt-out/fp4-sm120` D through a base-split construction: near-deterministic sub-screen spikes plus a 2:4 residual on the sparse FP4 instruction, about 24–40% of γ₀ at zero debit. NVFP4's int8 closure is D through scale flatness. Both are derived, not yet end to end; the assessor replays them on the card. bc-a8466279 leads the fix (`internal/pouw/red-team/ratings.md`, 11:55Z and 12:00Z). **Update 14:06Z: bc-a8466279's fix F1′ + F2 is a conditional GO** (the assessor, `internal/pouw/red-team/fp4-basesplit-fix-review.md`), pending the assessor's int8-Strassen replay on the card. The lines stay flagged and off the plots until that replay stands). Their rows stay in the attempts table below.*

| 63 | pearl-c-sm120 | v1-h3 | prefill (`m8192-n8192-k8192:hashing-only`) | measured | 1.28× | being derived | kernel | **hashing only** (around a stand-in GEMM, not the protocol's total; off the plots): Measured on GPU 1's real call at 8,192^3 (sm120): -h3t (A's segment leaves, segment trees and row seeds in its producer), with -h1's message digests fused into GPU 1's gemm_pearlc_b epilogue (its branch at 507e6aa1, built by its own build.sh and unedited). That epilogue is lockstep: all 8 consumer warps hash after each tile's last MMA, and the kernel has no staggered form, so the estimate's staggered lo end (1.148x/1.144x) is not measured. The tile leaves and the frame-b3s tile tree follow the GEMM. Hashing 0.418 ms: the arm's 2.7188 ms less the same call without hashing (2.3009 ms, gemm_pearlc_s), each a CUDA graph, interleaved with an L2 flush before each. The baseline is GPU 1's fastest plain FP8 GEMM in the same run (gemm_fast, 1.5053 ms); over cuBLASLt's 1.4457 ms (GPU 6's, another run) this row would be 1.2890x. Unfused (gemm_pearlc storing C~ and U, then hash_msg_v): 1.5058x. Parts, from r20260930-130051-8e6f on another die, whose totals match within 0.001 ms: digests in the epilogue 0.251 ms (GPU 2's lockstep estimate 0.21), A's commitment in the producer 0.035, A's tree and seed 0.043, tile leaves and tree 0.104. The estimate had no tree terms. E_A's lines use seed_line_a's one key as a timing stand-in for -h3's per-row keys (the same keyed hash per row). The GPU was held on a preemptible fill lease while fill jobs ran on the other GPUs. Every output the transcript reads was poisoned before each arm's verified replay, which starts from its own y; the negative control was rejected. | 69 | pearl-c-sm120 | v1-h3 | prefill (`m8192-n8192-k8192:hashing-only`) | measured | 1.22× | being derived | kernel | **hashing only** (around a stand-in GEMM, not the protocol's total; off the plots): Measured on GPU 1's real call at 8,192^3 (sm120), in 16-call chains: attempt 63's -h3t with A's tree and seed moved beside forming (a side stream at the chain's priority, started once the producer is done: under -h3 forming needs only the producer's row seeds), and each call's tile leaves and tile tree beside the next call's producer and forming (a side stream at the chain's priority, into one of two slots that call i + 2's GEMM waits for). The kernels and bytes are the same. Beside forming, hash_tree_small runs on 128 threads above 256 nodes, because at 1,024 threads of 64 registers it needs a whole SM. Hashing is 0.326 ms per call, against 0.411 ms for the same calls in series in the same run (h3s; attempt 63's single call measured about the same). That hides 58% of the 0.147 ms of A's tree, seed and tile hashing (the estimate was about 0.15 ms, 1.18x). The tile stream at low priority measures 1.2268x in the same run. The last call's tile hashing isn't overlapped and counts, about 0.0065 ms per call. The baseline is GPU 1's fastest plain FP8 GEMM in the run (gemm_fast, 1.4971 ms, in single-GEMM reps). E_A's lines use a fixed stand-in key in place of -h3's per-row keys (the same keyed hash per row). The GPU (GPU 2) was held on a preemptible fill lease while fill jobs ran on the other GPUs. Every output the last call's transcript reads was poisoned before the replay, and that call's y is the arm's own; the negative control was rejected. | 73 | pearl-c-sm120 | v1-h3 | prefill (`m8192-n8192-k8192:hashing-only`) | measured | 1.23× | being derived | kernel | **hashing only** (around a stand-in GEMM, not the protocol's total; off the plots): A negative result. Attempt 69's family of schedules, with the GEMM's digests kept in L2 for the tile hashing. An access-policy window on each gemm_pearlc_b graph node marks its 64 MB of digest writes persisting (a 64 MB set-aside of the 128 MB L2; 80 MB is the card's maximum), so hash_leaf_p reads them from L2 instead of DRAM. It is a launch attribute only. Hashing is 0.346 ms against 0.321 without the window in the same run (h3o), and 0.364 with the tile stream at the chain's priority. An nsys trace (r20260930-135845-2627, -h3) shows why: the producer gains 18 us a call because the reread hits L2, but the persisting lines crowd everyone else's L2. The GEMM loses 33 us, form_s5 23, gemm_words 13 and stats_s5 11. The whole run had the set-aside configured. The GPU was held on a preemptible fill lease while fill jobs ran on the other GPUs. The verifier checked the last call's transcript, from its own y after every output it reads was poisoned; the negative control was rejected. Hashing only, on the real GEMM, per call of 16-call chains | r20260930-135625-272a (node 2, GPU-2b59d5fe, preemptible fill lease); cursor/pearl-c-prefill-sidefix-b0c4 19e60934; GPU 1's cursor/pearl-c-sm120-h1-b44b 507e6aa1; internal/pouw/rtx-pro/a-commit-latency.md §11 `r20260930-135625-272a` |

| 81 | pearl-c-sm120 | v1-h3 | decode (`m32-n8192-k8192:hashing-only`) | measured | 2.25× | being derived | kernel | **hashing only** (around a stand-in GEMM, not the protocol's total; off the plots): Measured on GPU 1's real decode call (m = 32 of its 64 x 64 pipeline, gemm_pearlc_d unfused, then hash_msg_v), a 32-call dependent chain: call i's A is derived from call i - 1's y, and four weight sets are cycled so the weights never sit in L2. -h3t with the coalesced producer (segment leaves, trees and row seeds fused), A's tree and seed, then hash_msg_v, the tile leaves and the frame-b3s tile tree, all in series. Hashing is 62.5 us a call: the call less the same call without hashing (119.0 us a call, with the chain's usual producer). This is the first decode row on the real call; the stand-in GEMM's was 1.3104x (attempt 57, tile hashing deferred). E_A's lines use a fixed stand-in key in place of -h3's per-row keys; the dependency on the row seeds is kept. The baseline is cuBLASLt's FP8 GEMM at m = 32 on the same four weight sets in the same run (49.9 us a call), autotuned over its heuristic's 8 algorithms; the adopted decode divisor is 52.7 us. The GPU (GPU 7) was held on a preemptible fill lease while fill jobs ran on the other GPUs.
